## Summary

These contracts accept signatures from a key that a function can replace. 1,399 code families, 1,490,483 addresses, $6.8B held, 1,085,911 recoveries in the study's two-week window. Rows by rating: LOW 1,399. 635 rows were checked on a real transaction; the others were read from source, judged from bytecode or placed by measured rules (each row's note says which).

## Mechanism

PQ-17. Once that key's account has real code its signatures stop counting; its holder rotates first, or the owner afterwards.

## Evidence

- Each row's note says how it was decided: read from verified source, judged from bytecode and function names, placed by a rule measured against hand readings, or executed: a real transaction replayed on the fork's reference implementation with only the precompile changed, state at block 26,438,999.
- 3,072-gas wallets (factory `0xcd097b8dd0505aed5ed3f6a02416b0fb9e19fc59`): bytecode pc 187–207 `PUSH2 0x0c00`, `STATICCALL` to `0x01`, `INVALID` on failure. Executed: transaction `0x0323ebe3274b24b64ec6193d93729c9a37fecb1371d921cfd6d7dc98628dbd0f` (block 26,432,607) succeeds today with 64,082 gas, as its receipt says; under EIP-8151 it hits `INVALID` and uses the whole 200,000.
- tBTC v2 WalletRegistry `0x46d52e41c2f300bc82217ce22b920c34995204eb`, validator `0x1a5f4f753bd071c907714aefb6e0bcee32401406` (`tryRecover`, false on mismatch). Executed on the last real DKG result: with one byte of code at operator `0xb71c49e11048bf3d2bc0bab81252334295dee012`, approval `0xdad127784152301e0be04a5d5bd40d676ab85f4a707331f0b82bd2323d9eab28` reverts and a challenge by an outsider succeeds with `DkgMaliciousResultSlashed(400 T, submitter)`. Today `seize` only emits an event.
- ETHEscrowV2 `0x3a362a1966ff5aab35298e1565bda2c9fb124e41` and ERC20EscrowV2 `0xccb2718d633ed28bdf0639134380e2f5b05162b5`: `resolveDispute` requires `signer == buyer || signer == seller`. Executed: resolutions `0x34e6fa77b3850be07f6a71491781750c6a792c81c1ef5a51e29e02a91aa1b117` (ETH) and `0x47b43aced505820e8d2224d965b3bdf2c6c8b079b64f7ff0ef7228f210482ea0` (USDT) revert with code at the signing party; the escrow then waits 180 days for the operator's sweep.

## Impact and exposure

Largest rows by value held at block 26,438,999:
- `0xce43851485f437005ef3c1821e250c4d3c53b946` (SingleOwnerMSCA): $4.2B, LOW, 7,712 addresses
- `0x9512a1ff49fcceaa464ae44226a004ba19122518` (SingleOwnerMSCA): $1.7B, LOW, 40 addresses
- `0x2ceb3e994a516c6ba8658211bc94d4c280486cb7` (GnosisSafeL2): $226.2M, LOW, 8,589 addresses
- `0x642c9b393c3754d3618b88e16d6c96c13a467627` (WalletCore): $121.2M, LOW, 106,596 addresses
- `0x1a446a47aed68ef4f9b1536c5f8f27f9dc03f508` (unverified): $117.5M, LOW, 3,696 addresses
- `0xf4ac5a5dc1543086347d28e757091ebd1b848cca` (SafeL2): $88.5M, LOW, 39,586 addresses
- `0x0cfabe8a8a5cd4ceee2baaa850cfb92f7391f47e` (SemiModularAccount7702): $88.1M, LOW, 98,841 addresses
- `0x0a4fd9ebe5d8ebf63b1ab77a3296e2d79884b48e` (GnosisSafe): $46.8M, LOW, 965 addresses
- `0xea6294f6d4b231141f04562887a0d06f8df3bd49` (CaliburEntry): $35.7M, LOW, 47,265 addresses
- `0x22becbd1958d25536b7a08bbc12e6048df4ab9a3` (ZtakingPool): $35.2M, LOW, 3 addresses

Every row is in the header; `generated/triage_contracts.csv` lists them for search.

## Remediation

Rotate the key before its account takes code.
